It writes two transactions in category `TRANSFER` and prints both rows. Either both rows are stored or neither is.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- Exactly four arguments; the wrong count is a usage error handled by the CLI usage contract.\n- Validate before the first write: the amount through `validation.parse_amount` (integer cents) and the date through `validation.parse_date`. The amount must be positive. `<from>` and `<to>` must differ. Both accounts must exist. The category `TRANSFER` must exist. Every violation raises `Rejected` (exit 3).\n- Read the largest stored `txn_id` (last row of `transactions.list`, or none). The outgoing row takes that id plus one, and the incoming row the next id.\n- Outgoing row: account `<from>`, amount `-amount`, category `TRANSFER`, memo is the words `transfer to` followed by a space and `<to>`.\n- Incoming row: account `<to>`, amount `+amount`, category `TRANSFER`, memo is the words `transfer from` followed by a space and `<from>`.\n- Call `balance_rules.check_final_balances` with both pending inserts so the source account is never left below zero. Rejection exits 3 and writes nothing.\n- The id read, the balance check and both inserts run in one database transaction, so a concurrent command cannot take a balance below zero between check and write or reuse an id.\n- Output: `txn_id,date,account,category,amount,memo` header, then the outgoing row and the incoming row, with amounts written by `csv_output`.\n- Total cash held is unchanged by a transfer.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- `FEATURE-TXN-001` — write: insert the outgoing and incoming rows - supplies: `txn_id`, `date`, `account`, `category`, `amount`, `memo` - returns: `txn_id`, `date`, `account`, `category`, `amount`, `memo`\n- `FEATURE-TXN-003` — read: list transactions to find the largest id and an account balance - supplies: `account` - returns: `txn_id`, `date`, `account`, `category`, `amount`, `memo`\n- `FEATURE-ACCOUNT-002` — read: confirm an account exists - supplies: `account` - returns: `account`, `name`\n- `FEATURE-CATEGORY-002` — read: confirm category TRANSFER exists - supplies: `category` - returns: `category`, `description`\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC transfer-success ===\nIntent: A valid transfer stores consecutive TRANSFER rows, |\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261026 V1 |\n| Description | The `ledger report balances` command that reports each account's transaction count and balance computed from stored data. |\n| Depends On | FEATURE-LoadTransactions.md, FEATURE-Transfer.md, FEATURE-CsvOutput.md, DATABASE.md |\n| Provides | ledger report balances |\n| Consumes | ledger.register_command, persistence.open_database, csv_output.write_rows, csv_output.format_cents |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\n`ledger report balances` writes one row per account with its transaction count and balance, then a total row. Every value is computed from stored transactions in integer cents.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- Header: `account,name,transactions,balance`.\n- One row per account ordered by code in byte order (digits before letters), including accounts with no transactions (count 0, balance 0.00).\n- `balance` is the sum of the account's transaction amounts in integer cents, formatted with exactly two decimals and a leading `-` when negative.\n- Final row: `TOTAL,,<count>,<sum>` where `<count>` is the total number of transactions and `<sum>` the sum of all balances. A zero total is `0.00`, never `-0.00`.\n- On an empty ledger the output is the header and `TOTAL,,0,0.00`.\n- No value is embedded; everything is derived from `accounts.list` and `transactions.list`.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- `FEATURE-ACCOUNT-003` — read: list accounts ordered by code - returns: `account`, `name`\n- `FEATURE-TXN-003` — read: list every transaction to compute counts and balances - returns: `txn_id`, `date`, `account`, `category`, `amount`, `memo`\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC report-balances-seeded ===\nIntent: Balances over the loaded seed data match values derived from the seed files, including an unused account sorted first.\n\nimport csv\nimport io\nimport re\nfrom decimal import Decimal\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\nwith open(\"sources/seed_accounts.csv\", newline=\"\") as fh:\n    accounts = {r[\"account\"]: r[\"name\"] for r in csv.DictReader(fh)}\nwith open(\"sources/seed_transactions.csv\", newline=\"\") as fh:\n    txns = list(csv.DictReader(fh))\n\nextra_code, extra_name = \"0EMPTY\", \"Unused Account\"\n\nfor args in (\n    (\"init\",),\n    (\"load\", \"accounts\", \"sources/seed_accounts.csv\"),\n    (\"load\", \"categories\", \"sources/seed_categories.csv\"),\n    (\"load\", \"transactions\", \"sources/seed_transactions.csv\"),\n):\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(*args)\n    print(out, err)\n    assert code == 0\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.accounts.create(account=extra_code, name=extra_name)\naccounts[extra_code] = extra_name\n\ncounts = {c: 0 for c in accounts}\nsums = {c: 0 for c in accounts}\nfor t in txns:\n    counts[t[\"account\"]] += 1\n    sums[t[\"account\"]] += int(Decimal(t[\"amount\"]) * 100)\n\ncode, out, err = cli_runner(\"report\", \"balances\")\nprint(out, err)\nassert code == 0\nassert \"\\r\" not in out and out.endswith(\"\\n\")\nrows = list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(out)))\nassert rows[0] == [\"account\", \"name\", \"transactions\", \"balance\"]\ncodes = sorted(accounts)\nassert [r[0] for r in rows[1:-1]] == codes\nassert codes[0] == extra_code\nfor r in rows[1:-1]:\n    assert BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-Transfer.md ===\n# FEATURE: Transfer\n\n| Field | Value |\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261026 V1 |\n| Description | The `ledger transfer` command that records a paired debit and credit in category TRANSFER atomically. |\n| Depends On | FEATURE-TxnPost.md, FEATURE-BalanceRules.md, FEATURE-Validation.md, FEATURE-CsvOutput.md, DATABASE.md |\n| Provides | ledger transfer |\n| Consumes | ledger.register_command, persistence.open_database, validation.parse_amount, validation.parse_date, balance_rules.check_final_balances, csv_output.write_rows, ledger_errors.Rejected |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\n`ledger transfer <from> <to> <amount> <date>` moves cash between two distinct accounts. It writes two transactions in category `TRANSFER` and prints both rows. Either both rows are stored or neither is.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- Exactly four arguments; the wrong count is a usage error handled by the CLI usage contract.\n- Validate before the first write: the amount through `validation.parse_amount` (integer cents) and the date through `validation.parse_date`. The amount must be positive. `<from>` and `<to>` must differ. Both accounts must exist. The category `TRANSFER` must exist. Every violation raises `Rejected` (exit 3).\n- Read the largest stored `txn_id` (last row of `transactions.list`, or none). The outgoing row takes that id plus one, and the incoming row the next id.\n- Outgoing row: account `<from>`, amount `-amount`, category `TRANSFER`, memo is the words `transfer to` followed by a space and `<to>`.\n- Incoming row: account `<to>`, amount `+amount`, category `TRANSFER`, memo is the words `transfer from` followed by a space and `<from>`.\n- Call `balance_rules.check_final_balances` with both pending inserts so the source account is never left below zero. Rejection exits 3 and writes nothing.\n- The id read, the balance check and both inserts run in one database transaction, so a concurrent command cannot take a balance below zero between check and write or reuse an id.\n- Output: `txn_id,date,account,category,amount,memo` header, then the outgoing row and the incoming row, with amounts written by `csv_output`.\n- Total cash held is unchanged by a transfer.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- `FEATURE-TXN-001` — write: insert the outgoing and incoming rows - supplies: `txn_id`, `date`, `account`, `category`, `amount`, `memo` - returns: `txn_id`, `date`, `account`, `category`, `amount`, `memo`\n- `FEATURE-TXN-003` — read: list transactions to find the largest id and an account balance - supplies: `account` - returns: `txn_id`, `date`, `account`, `category`, `amount`, `memo`\n- `FEATURE-ACCOUNT-002` — read: confirm an account exists - supplies: `account` - returns: `account`, `name`\n- `FEATURE-CATEGORY-002` — read: confirm category TRANSFER exists - supplies: `category` - returns: `category`, `description`\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC transfer-success ===\nIntent: A valid transfer stores consecutive TRANSFER rows, reads back, and conserves total cash.\n\nimport csv\nimport io\nfrom decimal import Decimal\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\nsrc, dst = \"DRAWER1\", \"SAFE\"\ndeposit = \"100.00\"\namount = \"40.25\"\nday = \"2026-04-03\"\n\nfor args in (\n    (\"init\",),\n    (\"load\", \"categories\", \"sources/seed_categories.csv\"),\n    (\"load\", 0\n\ndef total(db):\n    return sum(r.amount for r in db.transactions.list())\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    ids_before = [r.txn_id for r in db.transactions.list()]\n    cash_before = A zero total is `0.00`, never `-0.00`.\n- On an empty ledger the output is the header and `TOTAL,,0,0.00`.\n- No value is embedded; everything is derived from `accounts.list` and `transactions.list`.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- `FEATURE-ACCOUNT-003` — read: list accounts ordered by code - returns: `account`, `name`\n- `FEATURE-TXN-003` — read: list every transaction to (\"load\", \"categories\", \"sources/seed_categories.csv\"),\n    (\"load\", \"transactions\", \"sources/seed_transactions.csv\"),\n):\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(*args)\n    print(out, err)\n
